### SD2:12
Nearly six full moons have passed since your heroic adventuring band brought down a trio of self-styled Overlords which, as part of a secretive Inner Council of nine, conspired to weaken the Sword Lands in order to conquer this rich and lush region.

### SD2:13
But the Overlords' designs went beyond simple conquest; indeed, their aim was to turn the Sword Lands into a vast power base from which they would enslave all of the Realmz continent, and establish a new empire ruled by psionic beings like themselves.

### SD2:14
For the Overlords are, as you had found out, of the race of humans known as Keryndorhyans, a reclusive people who live hidden somewhere on the far-away eastern continent of Anarym. And these folks are themselves descendants of the fabled Lankharhans,

### SD2:15
the race of psionic humans that established and ruled the legendary Empire of Lankharh, which fell some nine millennia past. Thus, the Inner Council of Overlords sought nothing less than to bring about a second Lankharhan Empire on the Realmz continent.

### SD2:16
So it was understandable when everyone breathed a sigh of relief once you had defeated the trio of Overlords that operated in the Sword Lands. From the simple peasant to the most powerful Merchant Lord, all rejoiced and sung praises to you and the Fates.

### SD2:17
And all were quite content to go back to their normal lives, as they were. But you, and your adventurer's instincts, knew better. You could not forget the fateful words of a fourth Overlord you had spoken to after the final battle with the Overlord trio,

### SD2:18
which promised the coming of an all-out war in the Sword Lands. Since you had found out already that the Warlord of Sharranth, the last known ally of the Overlords in the Sword Lands, was building a large army poised to march out and sweep the area,

### SD2:19
you felt certain that the Overlord Conspiracy was far from over. Yet, no one would listen to you, not even the High Council of Tanirith.  Hence, you had taken upon yourselves to secretly watch Sharranth and its army for any signs of dangerous activity.

### SD2:20
A mere sevenday ago, you were sadly proven right: with deadly efficiency, Sharranth's army swarmed into the Heartlands through the town of Aranys, rapidly overtaking the mining town of Sparklestone. And now, it is already laying siege to Wailsrond.

### SD2:21
After days of evading and fighting sharranthine patrols, you have finally made it back to the Eastern lands, near the great city of Tanirith. Bloodied and tired, only one thing drives you:

### SD2:22
To your dismay, one of Sharranth's pursuing patrols have caught up with you, seeking to prevent you from warning Tanirith of the invasion!

### SD2:23
You manage to flee for your lives!!!

### SD2:24
To your relief, a passing patrol of knights of the Flame rides fast to your help!

### SD2:25
After the clash, the commander of the knight patrol comes up to you smiling: "Well fought, Heroes! We're honored to have been of help to you! Just who are those hoodlums who've attacked you? And I noticed that they be mixed in with minotaurs and ogres?"

### SD2:26
You explain to him that this band was but a sample of Sharranth's mercenary army which has invaded the Heartlands. Only then does the commander see your caked wounds and dirtied vestments. "By Tyr!" He whispers, a look of devastation crossing his face.

### SD2:27
"You were right all along, as High Commander Fireblade had told us!" He adds. You can't help but smile at the mention of your former comrade, who is now leading the Knighthood of the Flame. "Let us heal you, brave ones" offers the commander,

### SD2:28
"and then I'll ride to the Lord Paladine to tell him of your arrival; I guess correctly that you'll be heading directly to his castle?" As you nod in response, he and his men heal you as best as they can, then ride off to Tanirith.

### SD2:29
Nearly six full moons have passed since a dire conspiracy, which threatened all of the Sword Lands, was exposed and undone.  As natives of the area, you had heard all about the mysterious Overlords which, as part of a secretive Inner Council of nine,

### SD2:30
had sought to weaken the Sword Lands in order to conquer them, helped with traitorous accomplices whom had been promised positions of power once the lands were taken. But thanks to a heroic band of adventurers from Bywater, these plans had been foiled.

### SD2:31
For the Overlords are, as you came to understand, of the race of humans known as Keryndorhyans, a people who live hidden somewhere on the far-away eastern continent of Anarym. And these folks are themselves descendants of the fabled Lankharhans,

### SD2:32
So it was understandable when everyone breathed a sigh of relief once the trio of Overlords operating in the Sword Lands was defeated, despite all the troubles they had caused. From the peasant to the richest Merchant Lord, all sung praises to the Fates.

### SD2:33
And all were quite content to go back to their normal lives. But you, and your adventurer's instincts, knew better. Indeed, six other Overlords remained out there, somewhere. And having heard numerous tavern rumors that the Warlord of Sharranth,

### SD2:34
who is the last known ally of the Overlords in your lands, was building a large army poised to march out, you felt certain that the Overlord Conspiracy was in truth far from over. But no one would listen to you, let alone the Lord Paladine,

### SD2:35
or the High Council, of Tanirith. Hence, you had bravely taken upon yourselves to go to the Western region of your Sword Lands, in order to secretly watch the city of Sharranth and its army for any signs of suspicious or dangerous activity.

### SD2:36

### SD2:37
"Our High Commander, Lord Fireblade, told us about you and your warnings: you were right all along, as he suspected!" You can't help but smile at the thought that someone important like the leader of the knighthood of the Flame WAS listening after all.

### SD2:2331

### SD2:2332

### SD2:2335

### SD2:2337

### SD2:2338

### SD2:2339

### SD2:4105
Just as you pass through this door, disembodied spirits of elven maiden appear before you, eyeing you sternly. "Begone from here!" One of them intones. "These ruins are sacred to elves and should not be violated!" At that, the spirits disappear.

### SD2:4106
From the skeletons of elves and humans strewn about this place, a savage clash must have occurred here ages ago. What remains of the warriors' uniforms, armors and weapons is now mostly useless, testifying to the centuries gone since this happened.

### SD2:4107
A disembodied feminine voice suddenly booms throughout the hall: "INTRUDERS ARE NOT WELCOME HERE. THIS SACRED PLACE MUST BE PROTECTED!" At that, some of the elven skeletons rise up!

### SD2:4108
"DO YOUR DUTY!" The disembodied voice adds, before the skeletons rush to attack.

### SD2:4109
It would seem that the "residents" of this place are not of the welcoming kind indeed ...

### SD2:4110
"I told you." Muses Riel, with a distinctive edge of anger in his voice. "We should leave this place well enough alone. Powerful spirits, and righteous ones at that, are guarding these ruins!" He shakes his head in exasperation.

### SD2:4111
"YOU WERE WARNED!" The disembodied voice suddenly booms, as more elven skeletal warriors materialize before you!

### SD2:4112
More spirits appear!

### SD2:4113
You enter a large hall filled with book shelves. This place must have been some kind of library.

### SD2:4114
After the battle, you glance over to Riel. Your comrade is fuming but otherwise remains silent. Obviously, he is convinced that you should leave this place. The spirits are certainly trying their best to convince you of that.

### SD2:4115
A strange field of energy blocks access to the library. The field does not appear to be magical, and seems to engulf all of this area of the place.

### SD2:4116
This is a statue of Lore, God of Knowledge and Wisdom.

### SD2:4117
This is an old and rusty ceremonial urn, often used for burning incense. You notice half-burnt scraps of parchment sheets within the urn, amidst what looks like fresh ashes. Someone recently burned parchment in this urn ... but to what purpose?

### SD2:4118
You drop a few sheets of parchment in the urn.

### SD2:4119
Having put parchment sheets in the urn, what do you want to do next?

### SD2:4120
You pour the contents of one of your flasks of oil over the parchment sheets.

### SD2:4121
Having doused the parchment sheets with flammable oil, what do you want to do next?

### SD2:4122
Hitting your weapon against the rim of the urn, you create sparks that ignite the oil-doused parchment sheets!

### SD2:4123
You ignite the oil-doused parchment sheets!

### SD2:4124
Shortly after you have lit the urn, a spirit appears next to the old throne.

### SD2:4125
This spirit bear the likeliness of an old elven scholar. He hovers a few inches above the florr, looking at you while motioning you to walk over to him.

### SD2:4126
The urn is alight still.

### SD2:4127
Without further due, the High Keeper disappears back whence he came.

### SD2:4128
At the same time, the fire in the urn dies out.

### SD2:4129
"DO NOT BOTHER TO SUMMON ME A SECOND TIME." Booms the disembodied voice of the High Keeper, in final parting.

### SD2:4130
The ashes of your fire lie within the urn.

### SD2:4131
"I am the High Keeper of the Lord Halls of Aurystan. Upon the fall of our peaceful kingdom at the hands of men, we swore that they would never acquire that which is our most cherished and valuable possession: our knowledge." He nods.

### SD2:4132
"That is why our books are not accessible to no one but other elven lore keepers, and so it shall be until the end of time. However, you have proven your mettle as well as your intelligence: therefore, I am willing to impart some of my knowledge."

### SD2:4133
What do you reply?

### SD2:4134
"We established the Forest-Kingdom of Aurystan some fifteen hundred years before the first men came to this region. Our home was located in what you call today the Harrow Woods, which surround the town of Aranys in these current times."

### SD2:4135
He chuckles dryly. "In our time, the forest of Aurystan was easily twice the size of its current state. When men began to arrive in the region some twelve hundred years ago, we befriended them. We even allowed eventually the construction of a road

### SD2:4136
passing through our kingdom, so that their cities may be linked for trade and free travel.  They had given us assurances that the villages and sacred places of our kingdom would never be trespassed. But then came the Scourge, the one you call Ptaraz."

### SD2:4137
He shakes his head in disgust: "The Scourge's army of bloodthirsty killers, aided by hordes of demons summoned by his consort Tanira, overran our forest-kingdom to pillage and lay it to waste. Thus, some seven centuries past, our kingdom fell and

### SD2:4138
my people fled the Sword Lands, in order to find a better place to live." The High Keeper hangs his head low at that.

### SD2:4139
The High Keeper lifts his head with pride: "While we were fighting the Scourge's armies, we managed to place seals -both magical and otherwise- to protect our most sacred places, such as our temples or our Hall of Lore. And those of us who died

### SD2:4140
while defending our positions or placing the seals, we pledged our souls to protect our sacred places even after death. Thus the ruins of our kingdom of Aurystan remained for centuries untouched and unexplored, unsoiled by looters and other pillagers."

### SD2:4141
"But some four centuries past came the Great Quake, which toppled to the ground nearly all that remained of our ruins. Then the year right after came a terrible battle within the forest, where powerful magics were used, obliterating our ruins as

### SD2:4142
well as most of the forest. In time, the trees, grasses and ferns came back, covering what little was left of our ruins, of the testimony of our past presence in this region. As you saw for yourselves, even constructions underground did not fare well

### SD2:4143
after such consequent disasters. Likewise, many of our seals were dissipated. I know for a fact that our Lore Hall is somewhat of an exception, however battered and crumbling as it is. In any event, we remain to guard our sacred places."

### SD2:4144
He chuckles: "I knew you would ask me such a question, for I am aware of the path you are currently walking upon. When my people settled in the forest of Aurystan of old, we found a strange, millennia-old man-made construction within the forest. This was

### SD2:4145
odd since men had not arrived yet to settle the Sword Lands. Eventually, some of us lost to their curiosity and dared to enter the ruins. Within, they found the remains of a man still dressed in his gear, lying next to a small, glowing talisman. His name

### SD2:4146
was Kith Khanaan. His living spirit, or essence, spoke to us through the talisman. He spoke of the War of the Mentarchs, or Mind Lords, and how it destroyed an empire of wonders. He told us about the Nine who were opposed by himself and two others,

### SD2:4147
and how five of the Nine were destroyed. He then told us of the ability of his kin to preserve one's essence, and that the last four of the Nine had done so. Fearing their eventual return, he crossed the oceans to hide the talismans that held their

### SD2:4148
essences. His nobility touched us, so we placed him in a shrine of his own in a remote place. I can tell you that his ruins, and even the four talismans, have been destroyed by the devastating quake and battle that decimated our own ruins also."

### SD2:4149
"So that is why Kith Khanaan left us and came here, nine thousand years ago." Naryl muses. "Even to this day, he refuses to answer this question. Now we know: he secreted the talismans of the last four surviving Mentarchs so that they never be found!"

### SD2:4150
She then chuckles: "And we now know who buried him in the Volcanic Rockies: the elves of old!" She shakes her head in amazement.

### SD2:4151
A half-drunk adventurer accosts you: "Hey, ye! I've got a map to some interesting ruins. My party is either gone or dead, and I don't feel like adventuring anymore. So I'll sell the map to ye fer 5 gems. Deal?"

### SD2:4152
"Don't want to spend or not enough money, eh? Then stop bothering me!"

### SD2:4153
He pockets the gems and hands you the map, before going back to his drink.
